   136	
   137	static bool open_proc(Proc & p, const std::string & path, int n_ctx, int threads, const std::string & npu_devs) {
   138	    llama_model_params mp = llama_model_default_params();
   139	    mp.load_mode = LLAMA_LOAD_MODE_NONE;  // as the Snapdragon docs run it (--load-mode none)
   140	    std::vector<ggml_backend_dev_t> devs;
   141	    if (p.name == "cpu") {
   142	        mp.n_gpu_layers = 0;
   143	    } else if (p.name == "gpu") {
   144	        ggml_backend_dev_t d = dev_by_prefix("GPUOpenCL");
   145	        if (!d) { p.error = "no OpenCL device"; return false; }
   146	        devs.push_back(d); mp.n_gpu_layers = -1;
   147	    } else {
   148	        std::stringstream ss(npu_devs); std::string n;
   149	        while (std::getline(ss, n, ',')) {
   150	            ggml_backend_dev_t d = dev_by_prefix(n.c_str());
   151	            if (!d) { p.error = "no device " + n; return false; }
   152	            devs.push_back(d);
   153	        }
   154	        mp.n_gpu_layers = -1;
   155	    }
   156	    devs.push_back(nullptr);  // CPU-only when the list holds nothing but the terminator
   157	    mp.devices = devs.data();
   158	    auto t0 = clk::now();
   159	    p.model = llama_model_load_from_file(path.c_str(), mp);
   160	    if (!p.model) { p.error = "model load failed"; return false; }
   161	    llama_context_params cp = llama_context_default_params();
   162	    cp.n_ctx = n_ctx; cp.n_batch = 512; cp.n_ubatch = 512;
   163	    cp.n_threads = threads; cp.n_threads_batch = threads;
   164	    cp.no_perf = true;
   165	    // The CPU instance must stay on the CPU: without this the scheduler may ship a large batch
   166	    // to any registered device that accepts it.
   167	    if (p.name == "cpu") cp.op_offload = false;
   168	    p.ctx = llama_init_from_model(p.model, cp);
   169	    if (!p.ctx) { p.error = "context init failed"; return false; }
   170	    p.load_ms = ms_since(t0);
   171	    return true;
   172	}
   173	
   174	static std::vector<llama_token> tokenize(const llama_vocab * v, const std::string & text, bool add_special) {
   175	    int n = -llama_tokenize(v, text.c_str(), text.size(), nullptr, 0, add_special, true);
   176	    std::vector<llama_token> t(n);
   177	    llama_tokenize(v, text.c_str(), text.size(), t.data(), n, add_special, true);
   178	    return t;
   179	}
   180	
   231	// Android's thermal HAL, read the way an unprivileged shell can: dumpsys thermalservice. Only the
   232	// "Current temperatures from HAL" block is read ("Cached" can be stale). AP is the application
   233	// processor's sensor and SKIN the case on Samsung's HAL. Each CPU policy's ceiling against its
   234	// hardware top is the CPU throttle; the Adreno ceiling is max_gpuclk against the top of
   235	// gpu_available_frequencies, where a shell may read them (-1 where it cannot).
   236	struct Heat { double ap = -1, skin = -1; int status = -1; long gpu_max_khz = -1, gpu_top_khz = -1, gpu_cur_khz = -1;
   237	    std::string cpu_max_mhz; bool cpu_capped = false; bool cpu_unreadable = false;
   238	    bool valid() const { return ap >= 0 && skin >= 0 && status >= 0 && !cpu_max_mhz.empty() && !cpu_unreadable; } };
   239	static std::string run_cmd(const char * cmd) {
   240	    std::string out; FILE * f = popen(cmd, "r");
   241	    if (!f) return out;
   242	    char buf[4096]; size_t n;
   243	    while ((n = fread(buf, 1, sizeof buf, f)) > 0) out.append(buf, n);
   244	    pclose(f);
   245	    return out;
   246	}
   247	static long gpu_top_khz() {
   248	    std::ifstream f("/sys/class/kgsl/kgsl-3d0/gpu_available_frequencies"); long v, top = -1;
   249	    while (f >> v) top = std::max(top, v / 1000);
   250	    return top;
   251	}
   252	static Heat heat(bool hal = true) {
   253	    Heat h;
   254	    std::string d = hal ? run_cmd("dumpsys thermalservice 2>/dev/null") : std::string();
   255	    size_t st = d.find("Thermal Status: ");
   256	    if (st != std::string::npos) h.status = atoi(d.c_str() + st + 16);
   257	    size_t cur = d.find("Current temperatures from HAL");
   258	    if (cur != std::string::npos) {
   259	        size_t endb = d.find("Current cooling devices", cur);
   260	        std::string blk = d.substr(cur, endb == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : endb - cur);
   261	        size_t i = 0;
   262	        while ((i = blk.find("mValue=", i)) != std::string::npos) {
   263	            double v = atof(blk.c_str() + i + 7);
   264	            size_t nm = blk.find("mName=", i), e = blk.find_first_of(",}", nm);
   265	            std::string name = nm == std::string::npos ? "" : blk.substr(nm + 6, e - nm - 6);
   266	            if (name == "AP") h.ap = v; else if (name == "SKIN") h.skin = v;
   267	            i = nm == std::string::npos ? i + 7 : nm;
   268	        }
   269	    }
   270	    for (int c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
   271	        std::string base = "/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpufreq/policy" + std::to_string(c);
   272	        long cap = read_long(base + "/scaling_max_freq"), top = read_long(base + "/cpuinfo_max_freq");
   273	        // A policy directory exists only for a cluster's first CPU; one that exists must read.
   274	        if (std::ifstream(base + "/related_cpus") && (cap <= 0 || top <= 0)) h.cpu_unreadable = true;
   275	        if (cap > 0 && top > 0 && cap < top) h.cpu_capped = true;
   276	        if (cap > 0) h.cpu_max_mhz += (h.cpu_max_mhz.empty() ? "" : ",") + std::to_string(c) + ":" + std::to_string(cap / 1000) + "/" + std::to_string(top / 1000);
   277	    }
   278	    static const long top = gpu_top_khz();
   279	    h.gpu_top_khz = top;
   280	    long mx = read_long("/sys/class/kgsl/kgsl-3d0/max_gpuclk"), cu = read_long("/sys/class/kgsl/kgsl-3d0/gpuclk");
   281	    h.gpu_max_khz = mx > 0 ? mx / 1000 : -1;
   282	    h.gpu_cur_khz = cu > 0 ? cu / 1000 : -1;
   283	    return h;
   284	}
   285	
   308	// AP and SKIN at or under the limit, thermal status 0 and no CPU ceiling lowered: the tablet's gate.
   309	static bool gate_ok(const Heat & h, double limit_c) {
   310	    return h.valid() && h.ap <= limit_c && h.skin <= limit_c && h.status == 0 && !h.cpu_capped;
   311	}
   312	static double cool_down(double limit_c, int max_s, Heat & at_start) {
   313	    auto t0 = clk::now();
   314	    while (true) {
   315	        at_start = heat();
   316	        if (gate_ok(at_start, limit_c) || ms_since(t0) / 1000 > max_s) return ms_since(t0) / 1000;
   317	        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds(5));
   318	    }
   319	}
   320	
   321	// Every HAL temperature, name=value, as one string.
   322	static std::string temps() {
   323	    std::string d = run_cmd("dumpsys thermalservice 2>/dev/null"), out;
   324	    size_t cur = d.find("Current temperatures from HAL");
   325	    if (cur == std::string::npos) return out;
   326	    size_t endb = d.find("Current cooling devices", cur), i = cur;
   327	    while ((i = d.find("mValue=", i)) != std::string::npos && (endb == std::string::npos || i < endb)) {
   328	        std::string v = d.substr(i + 7, d.find(',', i) - i - 7);
   329	        size_t nm = d.find("mName=", i), e = d.find_first_of(",}", nm);
   330	        out += d.substr(nm + 6, e - nm - 6) + "=" + v + " ";
   331	        i = nm;
   332	    }
   333	    return out;
   334	}
   335	
   336	// Feeds tokens in n_batch pieces; logits only for the last one.
   337	static bool feed(llama_context * ctx, const std::vector<llama_token> & toks, int pos0) {
   338	    int nb = llama_n_batch(ctx);
   339	    llama_batch b = llama_batch_init(nb, 0, 1);
   340	    for (size_t i = 0; i < toks.size(); i += nb) {
   341	        b.n_tokens = 0;
   342	        for (size_t j = i; j < toks.size() && j < i + nb; j++) {
   343	            b.token[b.n_tokens] = toks[j]; b.pos[b.n_tokens] = pos0 + (int) j;
   344	            b.n_seq_id[b.n_tokens] = 1; b.seq_id[b.n_tokens][0] = 0;
   345	            b.logits[b.n_tokens] = (j == toks.size() - 1);
   346	            b.n_tokens++;
   347	        }
   348	        if (llama_decode(ctx, b) != 0) { llama_batch_free(b); return false; }
   349	    }
   350	    llama_batch_free(b);
   398	
   399	    FILE * out = fopen(out_path.c_str(), "a");
   400	    if (!out) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot write %s\n", out_path.c_str()); return 2; }
   401	    int failures = 0;
   402	    const int ctx_used = window > 0 ? std::min(n_ctx, window + 256) : n_ctx;
   403	    long gguf_mb = -1;
   404	    { std::ifstream f(model_path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::ate); if (f) gguf_mb = (long) (f.tellg() / (1024 * 1024)); }
   405	    for (auto & [name, p] : procs) {
   406	        // Running out of memory restarted the MediaTek tablet twice (its NPU memory was kernel
   407	        // memory); refuse a copy that would leave less than the margin.
   408	        const long avail = mem_available_mb(), need = gguf_mb < 0 ? -1 : (long) (gguf_mb * 1.2) + mem_margin_mb;
   409	        if (avail < 0 || need < 0) p.error = "MemAvailable or the GGUF size unreadable: not loading without a memory check";
   410	        else if (avail < need) p.error = "insufficient memory: " + std::to_string(avail) + " MB available, " + std::to_string(need) + " MB needed";
   411	        else open_proc(p, model_path, ctx_used, threads, npu_devs);
   412	        fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"load\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"proc\":\"%s\",\"load_ms\":%.1f,\"ctx\":%d,\"avail_before_mb\":%ld,\"avail_after_mb\":%ld,\"error\":\"%s\"}\n",
   413	                esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), name.c_str(), p.load_ms, ctx_used, avail, mem_available_mb(), esc(p.error).c_str());
   414	        fflush(out);
   415	        fprintf(stderr, "loaded %s: %s %.0f ms\n", name.c_str(), p.error.empty() ? "ok" : p.error.c_str(), p.load_ms);
   416	        if (p.error.empty() && !warm(p.ctx, llama_model_get_vocab(p.model))) {
   417	            p.error = "warm-up failed";
   418	            fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"load\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"proc\":\"%s\",\"error\":\"warm-up failed\"}\n",
   419	                    esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), name.c_str());
   420	        }
   421	    }
   422	
   423	    std::vector<Conv> convs;
   424	    { std::ifstream f(conv_path); std::string line;
   425	      if (!f) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot read %s\n", conv_path.c_str()); return 2; }
   426	      while (std::getline(f, line) && (int) convs.size() < max_convs) if (!line.empty()) convs.push_back(parse_conv(line)); }
   427	
   428	    for (auto & [pn, dn] : cells) {
   429	        Proc & P = procs[pn]; Proc & D = procs[dn];
   430	        if (!P.error.empty() || !D.error.empty()) {
   431	            fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"cell_skipped\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"error\":\"%s\"}\n",
   432	                    esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), esc(P.error + " " + D.error).c_str());
   433	            fflush(out); failures++; continue;
   434	        }
   435	        Heat h0; double waited = cool_limit > 0 ? cool_down(cool_limit, cool_max_s, h0) : (h0 = heat(), 0.0);
   436	        fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"cooled\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"limit_c\":%.0f,\"max_s\":%d,\"gate\":\"%s\",\"waited_s\":%.0f,\"ap_c\":%.1f,\"skin_c\":%.1f,\"status\":%d,\"gpu_max_mhz\":%ld,\"gpu_top_mhz\":%ld,\"cpu_max_mhz\":\"%s\"}\n",
   437	                esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), cool_limit, cool_max_s, cool_limit <= 0 ? "off" : !h0.valid() ? "read_error" : gate_ok(h0, cool_limit) ? "passed" : "timed_out", waited, h0.ap, h0.skin, h0.status, h0.gpu_max_khz / 1000, h0.gpu_top_khz / 1000, h0.cpu_max_mhz.c_str());
   438	        fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"cell\",\"when\":\"start\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"gen\":%d,\"ctx\":%d,\"threads\":%d,\"max_turns\":%d,\"npu_devices\":\"%s\",\"temps\":\"%s\"}\n",
   439	                esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), gen, n_ctx, threads, max_turns, npu_devs.c_str(), temps().c_str());
   440	        fflush(out);
   441	        const llama_vocab * vocab = llama_model_get_vocab(P.model);
   442	        int n_vocab = llama_vocab_n_tokens(vocab);
   443	        std::vector<uint8_t> state, snap;
   444	        auto row_err = [&](const Conv & conv, int turn, const std::string & e) {
   445	            fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"turn_error\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"conv\":\"%s\",\"turn\":%d,\"error\":\"%s\"}\n",
   446	                    esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), esc(conv.id).c_str(), turn, esc(e).c_str());
   447	            fflush(out);
   448	            failures++;
   449	        };
   450	        const int limit = window > 0 ? std::min(n_ctx, window) : n_ctx;
   451	        auto span = [&](size_t n) { return (int) ((n + pad_batch - 1) / pad_batch * pad_batch); };
   452	        for (auto & conv : convs) {
   453	            llama_memory_clear(llama_get_memory(P.ctx), true);
   454	            if (&D != &P) llama_memory_clear(llama_get_memory(D.ctx), true);
   455	            std::vector<Msg> hist;
   456	            std::vector<llama_token> evaluated;  // exactly what the prefill context holds
   457	            int turn = 0;
   458	            for (size_t mi = 0; mi < conv.msgs.size() && turn < max_turns; mi++) {
   459	                hist.push_back(conv.msgs[mi]);
   460	                if (conv.msgs[mi].role != "user") continue;
   461	                std::string full = render(P.model, hist, true);
   462	                if (full.empty()) { row_err(conv, turn, "chat template failed"); break; }
   463	                std::vector<llama_token> all = tokenize_prompt(vocab, full);
   464	                // Teacher forcing only works if the new prompt extends what is cached, token for token.
   465	                if (all.size() <= evaluated.size() || !std::equal(evaluated.begin(), evaluated.end(), all.begin())) {
   466	                    row_err(conv, turn, "token prefix changed"); break;
   467	                }
   468	                std::vector<llama_token> toks(all.begin() + evaluated.size(), all.end());
   469	                const int pos = (int) evaluated.size();
   470	                int ref_tokens = -1, turn_gen = gen;
   471	                if (gen_ref) {
   472	                    // The reply as plain text, without template markup; +1 stands for its end of turn.
   473	                    if (mi + 1 < conv.msgs.size() && conv.msgs[mi + 1].role == "assistant")
   474	                        ref_tokens = (int) tokenize(vocab, conv.msgs[mi + 1].content, false).size() + 1;
   475	                    turn_gen = ref_tokens < 0 ? std::min(gen, 64) : std::max(2, std::min(gen, ref_tokens));
   476	                }
   477	                // The tablet's rule, so both phones end a conversation at the same turn.
   478	                if (pos + std::max((int) toks.size(), span(toks.size())) + turn_gen + 8 > limit) break;
   479	                std::string err;
   480	
   481	                // On the same processor a hybrid cannot drop the decoded tokens afterwards, so its
   482	                // state is saved before the prefill instead, outside both timed intervals.
   483	                const bool same = &D == &P;
   484	                // Only a model that cannot drop tokens needs the snapshot; a transformer drops the
   485	                // decoded tokens with seq_rm, so nothing touches its state before the timed prefill.
   486	                const bool needs_snap = same && (llama_model_is_hybrid(P.model) || llama_model_is_recurrent(P.model));
   487	                snap.clear();
   488	                if (needs_snap) {
   489	                    snap.resize(llama_state_seq_get_size(P.ctx, 0));
   490	                    if (snap.empty() || llama_state_seq_get_data(P.ctx, snap.data(), snap.size(), 0) != snap.size()) {
   491	                        row_err(conv, turn, "snapshot failed"); break;
   492	                    }
   493	                }
   494	
   495	                Heat hb = heat();  // before the turn, outside its timing
   496	                long wall_ms = (long) std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(std::chrono::system_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count();
   497	                auto t_turn = clk::now();
   498	                auto t0 = clk::now();
   499	                bool ok = feed(P.ctx, toks, pos);
   500	                const float * lg = ok ? llama_get_logits_ith(P.ctx, -1) : nullptr;
   501	                double prompt_ms = ms_since(t0);  // prompt evaluated and synchronised
   502	                llama_token first = 0;
   503	                if (lg) first = argmax(P.ctx, n_vocab); else { ok = false; err = "prefill failed"; }
   504	                double prefill_ms = ms_since(t0);  // plus choosing the first token
   505	
   506	                double get_ms = 0, set_ms = 0; size_t xfer_bytes = 0;
   507	                llama_context * dctx = D.ctx;
   508	                if (ok && !same) {
   509	                    xfer_bytes = llama_state_seq_get_size(P.ctx, 0);
   510	                    if (state.size() < xfer_bytes) state.resize(xfer_bytes);
   511	                    llama_memory_clear(llama_get_memory(D.ctx), true);
   512	                    auto tg = clk::now();
   513	                    size_t wrote = llama_state_seq_get_data(P.ctx, state.data(), xfer_bytes, 0);
   514	                    get_ms = ms_since(tg);
   515	                    auto ts = clk::now();
   516	                    size_t read = wrote == xfer_bytes ? llama_state_seq_set_data(D.ctx, state.data(), xfer_bytes, 0) : 0;
   517	                    set_ms = ms_since(ts);
   518	                    if (xfer_bytes == 0 || wrote != xfer_bytes || read != xfer_bytes) { ok = false; err = "state transfer failed"; }
   519	                }
   520	
   521	                std::vector<llama_token> out_toks;
   522	                std::vector<double> step_ms;
   523	                std::vector<int> step_threads;
   524	                double decode_ms = 0; int steps = 0;
   525	                // Only a CPU decoder has threads to change; the switch is read before each step and
   526	                // inside its timing, so a cell that switches pays for the reads it makes.
   527	                const bool switching = paused_threads > 0 && dn == "cpu";
   528	                int cur_threads = -1;
   529	                if (ok) {
   530	                    out_toks.push_back(first);
   531	                    llama_batch b = llama_batch_init(1, 0, 1);
   532	                    auto td = clk::now();
   533	                    int p2 = pos + (int) toks.size();
   534	                    for (int g = 1; g < turn_gen; g++) {
   535	                        auto tstep = clk::now();
   536	                        if (switching) {
   537	                            int p = cpu_paused(switches);
   538	                            if (p < 0) { ok = false; err = "pause switch unreadable"; break; }
   539	                            int want = p ? paused_threads : threads;
   540	                            if (want != cur_threads) { llama_set_n_threads(dctx, want, want); cur_threads = want; }
   541	                        }
   542	                        b.n_tokens = 1; b.token[0] = out_toks.back(); b.pos[0] = p2++;
   543	                        b.n_seq_id[0] = 1; b.seq_id[0][0] = 0; b.logits[0] = 1;
   544	                        if (llama_decode(dctx, b) != 0) { ok = false; err = "decode failed"; break; }
   545	                        out_toks.push_back(argmax(dctx, n_vocab));
   546	                        if (gen_ref) step_ms.push_back(ms_since(tstep));
   547	                        if (switching) step_threads.push_back(cur_threads);  // after success, so it lines up with step_ms
   548	                        steps++;
   549	                    }
   550	                    decode_ms = ms_since(td);
   551	                    llama_batch_free(b);
   552	                    // The next turn's prefill and warm-up use the cell's own count again.
   553	                    if (switching && cur_threads != threads) llama_set_n_threads(dctx, threads, threads);
   554	                }
   555	                double turn_ms = ms_since(t_turn);  // prefill, first token, handoff (with its clear) and decode
   556	                Heat ht = heat();  // at the end of the turn, outside its timing
   557	                if (same && ok) {
   558	                    // Put the context back to the dataset's history: drop the decoded tokens, or for
   559	                    // a hybrid restore the snapshot and read this turn's prompt again (untimed).
   560	                    llama_memory_t mem = llama_get_memory(P.ctx);
   561	                    if (!llama_memory_seq_rm(mem, 0, pos + (int) toks.size(), -1)) {
   562	                        if (snap.empty()) { ok = false; err = "cannot drop decoded tokens"; }
   563	                        llama_memory_clear(mem, true);
   564	                        if (llama_state_seq_set_data(P.ctx, snap.data(), snap.size(), 0) != snap.size() || !feed(P.ctx, toks, pos)) {
   565	                            ok = false; err = "restore failed";
   566	                        }
   567	                    }
   568	                    if (ok && llama_memory_seq_pos_max(mem, 0) != pos + (int) toks.size() - 1) { ok = false; err = "restore position wrong"; }
   569	                }
   570	
   571	                std::string step_list;
   572	                for (size_t k = 0; k < step_ms.size(); k++) {
   573	                    char buf[32]; snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "%s%.2f", k ? "," : "", step_ms[k]); step_list += buf;
   574	                }
   575	                std::string thr_list;
   576	                if (switching) {
   577	                    thr_list = "\"step_threads\":[";
   578	                    for (size_t k = 0; k < step_threads.size(); k++) thr_list += (k ? "," : "") + std::to_string(step_threads[k]);
   579	                    thr_list += "],";
   580	                }
   581	                std::string tok_list;
   582	                for (size_t k = 0; k < out_toks.size(); k++) tok_list += (k ? "," : "") + std::to_string(out_toks[k]);
   583	                int eog_at = -1;
   584	                for (size_t k = 0; k < out_toks.size(); k++) if (llama_vocab_is_eog(vocab, out_toks[k])) { eog_at = (int) k; break; }
   585	                std::string text;
   586	                for (int k = 0; k < (int) out_toks.size() && (eog_at < 0 || k < eog_at) && k < 48; k++) {
   587	                    char buf[256]; int n = llama_token_to_piece(vocab, out_toks[k], buf, sizeof buf, 0, false);
   588	                    if (n > 0) text.append(buf, n);
   589	                }
   590	                fprintf(out,
   591	                        "{\"kind\":\"turn\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"conv\":\"%s\",\"source\":\"%s\","
   592	                        "\"turn\":%d,\"pos\":%d,\"new_tokens\":%zu,\"prompt_ms\":%.2f,\"prefill_ms\":%.2f,\"xfer_get_ms\":%.2f,\"xfer_set_ms\":%.2f,\"xfer_bytes\":%zu,"
   593	                        "\"output_tokens\":%zu,\"decode_steps\":%d,\"decode_ms\":%.2f,\"turn_ms\":%.2f,\"wall_ms\":%ld,\"before_ap_c\":%.1f,\"before_skin_c\":%.1f,\"before_status\":%d,\"before_gpu_max_mhz\":%ld,\"before_cpu_capped\":%d,\"cpu_capped\":%d,\"before_cpu_max_mhz\":\"%s\",\"cpu_max_mhz\":\"%s\",\"ap_c\":%.1f,\"skin_c\":%.1f,\"status\":%d,\"gpu_max_mhz\":%ld,\"gpu_cur_mhz\":%ld,\"eog_at\":%d,\"error\":\"%s\",\"turn_gen\":%d,\"ref_tokens\":%d,%s\"step_ms\":[%s],\"tokens\":[%s],\"text\":\"%s\"}\n",
   594	                        esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), esc(conv.id).c_str(), esc(conv.source).c_str(),
   595	                        turn, pos, toks.size(), prompt_ms, prefill_ms, get_ms, set_ms, xfer_bytes, out_toks.size(), steps, decode_ms, turn_ms, wall_ms, hb.ap, hb.skin, hb.status, hb.gpu_max_khz / 1000, (int) hb.cpu_capped, (int) ht.cpu_capped, hb.cpu_max_mhz.c_str(), ht.cpu_max_mhz.c_str(), ht.ap, ht.skin, ht.status, ht.gpu_max_khz / 1000, ht.gpu_cur_khz / 1000, eog_at,
   596	                        esc(err).c_str(), turn_gen, ref_tokens, thr_list.c_str(), step_list.c_str(), tok_list.c_str(), esc(text).c_str());
   597	                fflush(out);
   598	                fprintf(stderr, "%s:%s %s t%d pos %d new %zu prompt %.0f ms xfer %.1f+%.1f ms decode %d steps in %.0f ms %s\n",
   599	                        pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), conv.id.c_str(), turn, pos, toks.size(), prompt_ms, get_ms, set_ms, steps, decode_ms, err.c_str());
   600	                if (!ok) { failures++; break; }
   601	                evaluated = all;
   602	                turn++;
   603	            }
   604	        }
   605	    }

    31	export type DuelRow = {
    32	  label: string;
    33	  // Who leads; a tie draws no bar.
    34	  who: "a" | "b" | "tie";
    35	  ratio?: number;
    36	  shown: string;
    37	  detail?: string;
    38	  // The other machine could not run it at all, so the bar is full length and striped, not measured.
    39	  blocked?: boolean;
    40	};

    42	// Who leads each test and by how much. A bar grows away from the center toward the machine that won. Lengths are on
    43	// a log scale, so 2× one way is as long as 2× the other.
    44	export function Duel({ a, b, groups, max }: { a: string; b: string; groups: { title?: string; rows: DuelRow[] }[]; max: number }) {
    45	  const room = "4.5rem";
    46	  const length = (r: DuelRow) => (r.blocked ? 1 : Math.log(r.ratio ?? 1) / Math.log(max));
    47	  return (
    48	    <ChartCard>
    49	      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 border-b border-rule pb-3 text-sm font-medium md:ml-[17rem]">
    50	        <span className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pr-3">
    51	          {a} leads
    52	          <Swatch color={seriesA} />
    53	        </span>
    54	        <span className="flex items-center gap-2 pl-3">
    55	          <Swatch color={seriesB} />
    56	          {b} leads
    57	        </span>
    58	      </div>
    59	      {groups.map((g, i) => (
    60	        <div key={g.title ?? i} className="mt-4">
    61	          {g.title && <p className="mb-1 text-sm font-medium">{g.title}</p>}
    62	          <ul>
    63	            {g.rows.map((r) => {
    64	              const bar = {
    65	                width: `calc((100% - ${room}) * ${length(r)})`,
    66	                backgroundColor: r.who === "a" ? seriesA : seriesB,
    67	              };
    68	              const stripes = r.blocked ? { backgroundImage: "repeating-linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,255,255,.45) 0 3px, transparent 3px 7px)" } : {};
    69	              return (
    70	                <li key={r.label} className="grid items-center gap-x-4 rounded py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-white md:grid-cols-[16rem_minmax(0,1fr)]">
    71	                  <div>
    72	                    <p className="text-sm leading-5">{r.label}</p>
    73	                    {r.detail && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-ink-soft">{r.detail}</p>}
    74	                  </div>
    75	                  <div className="mt-1 grid grid-cols-2 md:mt-0">
    76	                    <div className="flex h-5 items-center justify-end gap-1.5 border-r border-ink/25 pr-px text-xs tabular-nums">
    77	                      {r.who === "a" && (
    78	                        <>
    79	                          <span className="whitespace-nowrap font-medium">{r.shown}</span>
    80	                          <span className="h-2.5 rounded-l" style={{ ...bar, ...stripes }} />
    81	                        </>
    82	                      )}
    83	                      {r.who === "tie" && <span className="whitespace-nowrap pr-1 text-ink-soft">{r.shown}</span>}
    84	                    </div>
    85	                    <div className="flex h-5 items-center gap-1.5 pl-px text-xs tabular-nums">
    86	                      {r.who === "b" && (
    87	                        <>
    88	                          <span className="h-2.5 rounded-r" style={{ ...bar, ...stripes }} />

    95	              );
    96	            })}
    97	          </ul>
    98	        </div>
    99	      ))}
   100	    </ChartCard>
   101	  );
   102	}

   107	// Two machines on the same test. Each row has its own scale where the units differ between rows; `shared` puts them
   108	// on one scale where they do not. Where a result is a range, the bar is solid to the low end and faint up to the high end.
   109	export function PairBars({ a, b, shortA, shortB, rows, shared }: { a: string; b: string; shortA: string; shortB: string; rows: PairRow[]; shared?: boolean }) {
   110	  const all = Math.max(...rows.flatMap((r) => [r.a?.value ?? 0, r.b?.value ?? 0]));
   111	  return (
   112	    <ChartCard>
   113	      <Key a={a} b={b} />
   114	      <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-rule">
   115	        {rows.map((r) => {
   116	          const top = shared ? all : Math.max(r.a?.value ?? 0, r.b?.value ?? 0);
   117	          return (
   118	            <li key={r.label} className="grid gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 py-3 first:pt-0 last:pb-0 md:grid-cols-[16rem_minmax(0,1fr)]">
   119	              <div>
   120	                <p className="text-sm leading-5">{r.label}</p>
   121	                {r.detail && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-ink-soft">{r.detail}</p>}
   122	                {r.lead && <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs font-medium leading-5">{r.lead}</p>}
   123	              </div>
   124	              <div className="space-y-1">
   125	                <Bar reading={r.a} top={top} color={seriesA} name={shortA} missing={r.missing} />
   126	                <Bar reading={r.b} top={top} color={seriesB} name={shortB} missing={r.missing} />
   127	              </div>
   128	            </li>
   129	          );
   130	        })}
   131	      </ul>
   132	    </ChartCard>
   133	  );
   134	}

   136	function Bar({ reading, top, color, name, missing }: { reading: Reading; top: number; color: string; name: string; missing?: string }) {
   137	  if (!reading) {
   138	    return (
   139	      <p className="flex h-5 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-soft">
   140	        {name}: {missing ?? "not measured"}
   141	      </p>
   142	    );
   143	  }
   144	  const room = "11rem";
   145	  const full = reading.value / top;
   146	  const solid = (reading.low ?? reading.value) / reading.value;
   147	  return (
   148	    <div className="flex h-5 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs tabular-nums">
   149	      <span
   150	        className="relative h-2.5 rounded-r"
   151	        style={{ width: `calc((100% - ${room}) * ${full})`, minWidth: "2px", backgroundColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 35%, transparent)` }}
   152	      >
   153	        <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 rounded-r" style={{ width: `${solid * 100}%`, backgroundColor: color }} />
   154	      </span>
   155	      <span className="whitespace-nowrap">
   156	        <span className="font-medium">{name}</span> {reading.shown}
   157	      </span>
   158	    </div>
   159	  );
   160	}

    80	static std::string field(const std::string & obj, const std::string & key) {
    81	    size_t k = obj.find("\"" + key + "\"");
    82	    if (k == std::string::npos) return "";
    83	    size_t i = skip_ws(obj, obj.find(':', k) + 1);
    84	    return obj[i] == '"' ? read_str(obj, i) : "";
    85	}
    86	static Conv parse_conv(const std::string & line) {
    87	    Conv c; c.id = field(line, "id"); c.source = field(line, "source");
    88	    size_t m = line.find("\"messages\"");
    89	    size_t i = line.find('[', m) + 1;
    90	    while (true) {
    91	        i = skip_ws(line, i);
    92	        if (i >= line.size() || line[i] == ']') break;
    93	        if (line[i] == ',') { i++; continue; }
    94	        // one {"role":"..","content":".."} object, keys in either order
    95	        Msg msg; i++;
    96	        while (true) {
    97	            i = skip_ws(line, i);
    98	            if (line[i] == '}') { i++; break; }
    99	            if (line[i] == ',') { i++; continue; }
   100	            std::string key = read_str(line, i);
   101	            i = skip_ws(line, i) + 1;  // colon
   102	            i = skip_ws(line, i);
   103	            std::string val = read_str(line, i);
   104	            if (key == "role") msg.role = val; else if (key == "content") msg.content = val;
   105	        }
   106	        c.msgs.push_back(msg);
   107	    }
   108	    return c;
   109	}
   110	static std::string esc(const std::string & s) {
   111	    std::string o;
   112	    for (unsigned char ch : s) {
   113	        if (ch == '"' || ch == '\\') { o += '\\'; o += ch; }
   114	        else if (ch == '\n') o += "\\n"; else if (ch == '\r') o += "\\r"; else if (ch == '\t') o += "\\t";
   115	        else if (ch < 0x20) { char b[8]; snprintf(b, sizeof b, "\\u%04x", ch); o += b; }
   116	        else o += ch;
   117	    }
   118	    return o;
   119	}

   223	// The whole rendered prompt as tokens, with exactly one BOS where the vocabulary wants one and
   224	// no EOS after the generation header.
   225	static std::vector<llama_token> tokenize_prompt(const llama_vocab * v, const std::string & text) {
   226	    std::vector<llama_token> t = tokenize(v, text, true);
   227	    llama_token bos = llama_vocab_bos(v), eos = llama_vocab_eos(v);
   228	    while (t.size() >= 2 && t[0] == bos && t[1] == bos) t.erase(t.begin());
   229	    if (!t.empty() && t.back() == eos && llama_vocab_get_add_eos(v)) t.pop_back();
   230	    return t;
   231	}
   232	
   233	// Kernels compile and graphs reserve on first use (the OpenCL backend compiled its attention
   234	// kernels inside the first measured turn of the smoke run), so each context runs one full
   235	// batch and a few single-token steps before anything is timed.
   236	static bool warm(llama_context * ctx, const llama_vocab * v) {
   237	    std::vector<llama_token> t(512, llama_vocab_bos(v) >= 0 ? llama_vocab_bos(v) : 1);
   238	    llama_batch b = llama_batch_init(512, 0, 1);
   239	    b.n_tokens = 512;
   240	    for (int i = 0; i < 512; i++) { b.token[i] = t[i]; b.pos[i] = i; b.n_seq_id[i] = 1; b.seq_id[i][0] = 0; b.logits[i] = i == 511; }
   241	    bool ok = llama_decode(ctx, b) == 0;
   242	    for (int i = 0; i < 8 && ok; i++) {
   243	        b.n_tokens = 1; b.token[0] = t[0]; b.pos[0] = 512 + i; b.logits[0] = 1;
   244	        ok = llama_decode(ctx, b) == 0;
   245	    }
   246	    ok = ok && llama_get_logits_ith(ctx, -1) != nullptr;
   247	    llama_batch_free(b);
   248	    llama_memory_clear(llama_get_memory(ctx), true);
   249	    return ok;
   250	}

   256	// Sizes of an NPU export's model files (chunks and embedding) named in its runner.txt, in MB.
   257	static long npu_files_mb(const std::string & dir, long & files) {
   258	    std::ifstream f(dir + "/runner.txt"); std::string line; files = 0;
   259	    while (std::getline(f, line)) {
   260	        auto k = line.find('=');
   261	        if (k == std::string::npos) continue;
   262	        std::string key = line.substr(0, k), v = line.substr(k + 1);
   263	        if (key != "token_embedding_path" && key != "model_package_paths" && key != "prompt_model_paths" && key != "gen_model_paths") continue;
   264	        std::stringstream ss(v); std::string x;
   265	        while (std::getline(ss, x, ',')) { std::ifstream g(dir + "/" + x, std::ios::binary | std::ios::ate); if (g) files += (long) g.tellg() / (1024 * 1024); }
   266	    }
   267	    return 0;
   268	}

   277	// Android's thermal HAL, read the way an unprivileged shell can: dumpsys thermalservice. Its
   278	// "Cached temperatures" block can be stale, so only "Current temperatures from HAL" is read.
   279	// AP is the application-processor sensor and SKIN the case; the HAL gives no per-core, GPU or
   280	// NPU zone on this unit. Each CPU policy's ceiling against its hardware top is the CPU throttle,
   281	// and the GPU's ceiling is /sys/kernel/gpu/gpu_max_clock against the top of its frequency table.
   282	struct Heat { double ap = -1, skin = -1; int status = -1; long gpu_max_khz = -1, gpu_top_khz = -1, gpu_cur_khz = -1;
   283	    std::string cpu_max_mhz; bool cpu_capped = false; bool cpu_unreadable = false;
   284	    // Valid only when the HAL answered and every CPU policy's ceiling and top were read: an
   285	    // unreadable one must not pass as uncapped (Codex review, round two).
   286	    bool valid() const { return ap >= 0 && skin >= 0 && status >= 0 && !cpu_max_mhz.empty() && !cpu_unreadable; }
   287	    bool gpu_capped() const { return gpu_max_khz > 0 && gpu_top_khz > 0 && gpu_max_khz < gpu_top_khz; } };
   288	static std::string run_cmd(const char * cmd) {
   289	    std::string out; FILE * f = popen(cmd, "r");
   290	    if (!f) return out;
   291	    char buf[4096]; size_t n;
   292	    while ((n = fread(buf, 1, sizeof buf, f)) > 0) out.append(buf, n);
   293	    pclose(f);
   294	    return out;
   295	}
   296	static long gpu_top_khz() {
   297	    std::ifstream f("/sys/kernel/gpu/gpu_freq_table"); long v, top = -1;
   298	    while (f >> v) top = std::max(top, v);
   299	    return top;
   300	}
   301	// hal = false skips the thermal HAL (about a second of dumpsys) and reads only the clocks.
   302	static Heat heat(bool hal = true) {

   309	        size_t endb = d.find("Current cooling devices", cur);

   178	    std::vector<ggml_backend_dev_t> devs;
   179	    if (p.name == "cpu") {

   204	}
   205	
   206	static std::vector<llama_token> tokenize(const llama_vocab * v, const std::string & text, bool add_special) {
   207	    int n = -llama_tokenize(v, text.c_str(), text.size(), nullptr, 0, add_special, true);
   208	    std::vector<llama_token> t(n);
   209	    llama_tokenize(v, text.c_str(), text.size(), t.data(), n, add_special, true);
   210	    return t;
   302	static Heat heat(bool hal = true) {
   303	    Heat h;
   304	    std::string d = hal ? run_cmd("dumpsys thermalservice 2>/dev/null") : std::string();
   305	    size_t st = d.find("Thermal Status: ");
   306	    if (st != std::string::npos) h.status = atoi(d.c_str() + st + 16);
   307	    size_t cur = d.find("Current temperatures from HAL");
   308	    if (cur != std::string::npos) {
   309	        size_t endb = d.find("Current cooling devices", cur);
   310	        std::string blk = d.substr(cur, endb == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : endb - cur);
   311	        size_t i = 0;
   312	        while ((i = blk.find("mValue=", i)) != std::string::npos) {
   313	            double v = atof(blk.c_str() + i + 7);
   314	            size_t nm = blk.find("mName=", i), e = blk.find_first_of(",}", nm);
   315	            std::string name = nm == std::string::npos ? "" : blk.substr(nm + 6, e - nm - 6);
   316	            if (name == "AP") h.ap = v; else if (name == "SKIN") h.skin = v;
   317	            i = nm == std::string::npos ? i + 7 : nm;
   318	        }
   319	    }
   320	    for (int c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
   321	        std::string base = "/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpufreq/policy" + std::to_string(c);
   322	        long cap = read_long(base + "/scaling_max_freq"), top = read_long(base + "/cpuinfo_max_freq");
   323	        // A policy directory exists only for a cluster's first CPU; one that exists must read.
   324	        if (std::ifstream(base + "/related_cpus") && (cap <= 0 || top <= 0)) h.cpu_unreadable = true;
   325	        if (cap > 0 && top > 0 && cap < top) h.cpu_capped = true;
   326	        if (cap > 0) h.cpu_max_mhz += (h.cpu_max_mhz.empty() ? "" : ",") + std::to_string(c) + ":" + std::to_string(cap / 1000) + "/" + std::to_string(top / 1000);
   327	    }
   328	    static const long top = gpu_top_khz();
   329	    h.gpu_top_khz = top;
   330	    h.gpu_max_khz = read_long("/sys/kernel/gpu/gpu_max_clock");
   331	    h.gpu_cur_khz = read_long("/sys/kernel/gpu/gpu_clock");
   332	    return h;
   333	}
   334	
   335	// The CPU pause switches, named by core mask (thermal-pause-3C pauses cores 2 to 5, thermal-pause-C0
   336	// cores 6 and 7 on SM8850): while they are on, six decode threads crowd onto the two cores left (r19).
   337	// Both or none: one found alone would let a pause on the other go unseen.
   338	static std::vector<std::string> pause_switches() {
   339	    std::vector<std::string> out;
   340	    for (int c = 0; c < 400; c++) {
   341	        std::string base = "/sys/class/thermal/cooling_device" + std::to_string(c);
   342	        std::ifstream ft(base + "/type");
   365	    while (true) {
   366	        at_start = heat();
   367	        bool cool = gate_ok(at_start, limit_c);
   368	        if (cool || ms_since(t0) / 1000 > max_s) return ms_since(t0) / 1000;
   369	        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds(5));
   370	    }
   371	}
   372	
   373	// Every HAL temperature, name=value, as one string.
   374	static std::string temps() {
   375	    std::string d = run_cmd("dumpsys thermalservice 2>/dev/null"), out;
   376	    size_t cur = d.find("Current temperatures from HAL");
   377	    if (cur == std::string::npos) return out;
   378	    size_t endb = d.find("Current cooling devices", cur), i = cur;
   379	    while ((i = d.find("mValue=", i)) != std::string::npos && (endb == std::string::npos || i < endb)) {
   380	        std::string v = d.substr(i + 7, d.find(',', i) - i - 7);
   381	        size_t nm = d.find("mName=", i), e = d.find_first_of(",}", nm);
   382	        out += d.substr(nm + 6, e - nm - 6) + "=" + v + " ";
   383	        i = nm;
   384	    }
   385	    return out;
   520	    }
   521	    for (auto & name : order) {
   522	        Proc & p = procs[name];
   523	        const long avail = mem_available_mb();
   524	        const long need = name == "npu" ? npu_need_mb : gguf_mb * 12 / 10 + mem_margin_mb;
   525	        if (avail < 0) {
   526	            p.error = "MemAvailable unreadable: not loading without a memory check";
   527	        } else if (need > 0 && avail < need) {
   528	            p.error = "insufficient memory: " + std::to_string(avail) + " MB available, " + std::to_string(need) + " MB needed";
   529	        } else {
   530	            open_proc(p, model_path, ctx_used, threads, "", npu_dir);
   531	        }
   532	        if (p.npu) p.npu->rope_permute = rope_permute;
   533	        fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"load\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"proc\":\"%s\",\"load_ms\":%.1f,\"npu_dir\":\"%s\",\"ctx\":%d,\"avail_before_mb\":%ld,\"avail_after_mb\":%ld,\"error\":\"%s\"}\n",
   534	                esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), name.c_str(), p.load_ms, esc(p.npu ? npu_dir : "").c_str(), ctx_used, avail, mem_available_mb(), esc(p.error).c_str());
   535	        fflush(out);
   536	        fprintf(stderr, "loaded %s: %s %.0f ms\n", name.c_str(), p.error.empty() ? "ok" : p.error.c_str(), p.load_ms);
   537	        bool warmed = p.error.empty() && (p.npu ? warm_npu(*p.npu) : warm(p.ctx, llama_model_get_vocab(p.model)));
   538	        if (p.error.empty() && !warmed) {
   539	            p.error = "warm-up failed";
   540	            fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"load\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"proc\":\"%s\",\"error\":\"warm-up failed\"}\n",
   541	                    esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), name.c_str());
   542	        }
   543	        if (p.error.empty() && !p.npu) probe_layout(p);
   544	        // The vocabulary-only model carries no hyperparameters; a loaded one gives the sliding window.
   545	        if (p.error.empty() && !p.npu && procs.count("npu") && procs["npu"].npu && llama_model_n_swa(p.model) > 0) {
   546	            // The NPU's mask was built with the export's window; a different llama.cpp window would
   547	            // hand over cells the NPU then cannot see, or leave it attending to cells never filled.
   548	            if ((size_t) llama_model_n_swa(p.model) != procs["npu"].npu->n_swa)
   549	                p.layout_error = "sliding window differs: llama.cpp " + std::to_string(llama_model_n_swa(p.model)) + ", NPU export " + std::to_string(procs["npu"].npu->n_swa);
   550	        }
   551	    }
   552	
   553	    std::vector<Conv> convs;
   554	    { std::ifstream f(conv_path); std::string line;
   555	      if (!f) { fprintf(stderr, "cannot read %s\n", conv_path.c_str()); return 2; }
   556	      while (std::getline(f, line) && (int) convs.size() < max_convs) if (!line.empty()) convs.push_back(parse_conv(line)); }
   557	
   558	    // --check-handoff: the first turn of the first conversation read by the CPU and by the NPU;
   559	    // the NPU's cache converted to the CPU's layout is compared with the CPU's own, layer by
   560	    // layer (cosine of K and of V). A wrong head order, rope permutation or alignment shows as a
   561	    // cosine near zero; the two models' different quantisation as one a little under one.
   562	    if (check_handoff) {
   563	#ifdef PD_NPU
   564	        Proc & C = procs["cpu"]; Proc & N = procs["npu"];
   565	        if (!C.error.empty() || !N.error.empty() || !C.layout_error.empty()) { fprintf(stderr, "check: need cpu and npu: %s %s %s\n", C.error.c_str(), N.error.c_str(), C.layout_error.c_str()); return 2; }
   566	        // The history up to (and including) user turn check_turn of conversation check_conv, with the
   567	        // dataset's replies, as one prompt: long enough to cover positions past 512.
   568	        std::vector<Msg> h;
   569	        int seen = 0;
   570	        for (auto & m : convs[std::min<size_t>(check_conv, convs.size() - 1)].msgs) {
   571	            h.push_back(m);
   572	            if (m.role == "user" && seen++ == check_turn) break;
   573	        }
   574	        std::vector<llama_token> t = tokenize_prompt(vocab, render(vmodel, h, true));
   575	        // With --check-incremental the NPU reads each user turn's new tokens as its own prefill
   576	        // (right-padded pieces after the first), exactly as a cell's prefill side does.
   577	        std::vector<size_t> cuts;
   578	        if (check_incremental) {
   579	            std::vector<Msg> hh;
   580	            for (auto & m : h) {
   581	                hh.push_back(m);
   582	                if (m.role == "user") cuts.push_back(tokenize_prompt(vocab, render(vmodel, hh, true)).size());
   583	            }
   584	        }
   585	        feed(C.ctx, t, 0);
   586	        std::vector<uint8_t> bc(llama_state_seq_get_size(C.ctx, 0));
   587	        llama_state_seq_get_data(C.ctx, bc.data(), bc.size(), 0);
   588	        N.npu->clear();
   589	        if (cuts.size() > 1) {
   590	            size_t from = 0;
   591	            for (size_t c : cuts) { N.npu->prefill(std::vector<int32_t>(t.begin() + from, t.begin() + c)); from = c; }
   592	            fprintf(stderr, "incremental: %zu prefills, %zu tokens\n", cuts.size(), t.size());
   593	        } else {
   594	            N.npu->prefill(std::vector<int32_t>(t.begin(), t.end()));
   595	        }
   596	        std::vector<uint8_t> bn;
   597	        if (!N.npu->to_llama(C.layout, t.size(), bn)) { fprintf(stderr, "check: %s\n", N.npu->error.c_str()); return 2; }
   598	        npu::ParsedState sc, sn; std::string e;
   599	        if (!npu::parse_state(bc.data(), bc.size(), C.kind, sc, e) || !npu::parse_state(bn.data(), bn.size(), C.kind, sn, e)) { fprintf(stderr, "check: %s\n", e.c_str()); return 2; }
   600	        // Section by section, layer by layer, matched by position (the CPU's cells are in cache order).
   601	        for (size_t si = 0; si < sc.kv.size(); si++) {
   602	            const auto & A = sc.kv[si]; const auto & B = sn.kv[si];
   603	            if (A.pos.empty()) continue;
   604	            const size_t row = A.k_row[0] / 2;
   605	            std::map<int32_t, size_t> ia, ib;
   606	            for (size_t c = 0; c < A.pos.size(); c++) ia[A.pos[c]] = c;
   607	            for (size_t c = 0; c < B.pos.size(); c++) ib[B.pos[c]] = c;
   608	            for (size_t l = 0; l < A.n_layer; l++) {
   609	                double ck = 0, cv = 0; size_t m = 0;
   610	                // Also by position range, so an error that grows with position shows.
   632	            }
   633	        }
   634	        fclose(out);
   635	        fflush(stderr);
   636	        _exit(0);
   637	#else
   638	        fprintf(stderr, "built without PD_NPU\n"); return 2;
   639	#endif
   640	    }
   641	
   642	    for (auto & [pn, dn] : cells) {
   643	        Proc & P = procs[pn]; Proc & D = procs[dn];
   644	        const bool same = &D == &P;
   645	        std::string why = P.error.empty() && D.error.empty() ? "" : P.error + " " + D.error;
   646	        // A handoff touching the NPU needs an attention-only export and a plain llama.cpp cache
   647	        // on the other side (not hybrid, not sliding-window): see npu_engine.h.
   648	        if (why.empty() && !same && (P.npu || D.npu)) {
   649	            Proc & L = P.npu ? D : P;
   650	            if (!L.layout_error.empty()) why = "npu handoff: " + L.layout_error;
   651	        }
   652	        if (!why.empty()) {
   653	            fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"cell_skipped\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"error\":\"%s\"}\n",
   654	                    esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), esc(why).c_str());
   655	            fflush(out); failures++; continue;
   656	        }
   657	        Heat h0; double waited = cool_limit > 0 ? cool_down(cool_limit, cool_max_s, h0) : (h0 = heat(), 0.0);
   658	        fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"cooled\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"limit_c\":%.0f,\"max_s\":%d,\"gate\":\"%s\",\"waited_s\":%.0f,\"ap_c\":%.1f,\"skin_c\":%.1f,\"status\":%d,\"gpu_max_mhz\":%ld,\"gpu_top_mhz\":%ld,\"cpu_max_mhz\":\"%s\"}\n",
   659	                esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), cool_limit, cool_max_s, cool_limit <= 0 ? "off" : !h0.valid() ? "read_error" : gate_ok(h0, cool_limit) ? "passed" : "timed_out", waited, h0.ap, h0.skin, h0.status, h0.gpu_max_khz / 1000, h0.gpu_top_khz / 1000, h0.cpu_max_mhz.c_str());
   660	        fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"cell\",\"when\":\"start\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"gen\":%d,\"ctx\":%d,\"threads\":%d,\"max_turns\":%d,\"npu_dir\":\"%s\",\"temps\":\"%s\"}\n",
   661	                esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), gen, ctx_used, threads, max_turns, esc(npu_dir).c_str(), temps().c_str());
   662	        fflush(out);
   663	        std::vector<uint8_t> state, snap;
   664	        auto row_err = [&](const Conv & conv, int turn, const std::string & e) {
   665	            fprintf(out, "{\"kind\":\"turn_error\",\"label\":\"%s\",\"model\":\"%s\",\"prefill\":\"%s\",\"decode\":\"%s\",\"conv\":\"%s\",\"turn\":%d,\"error\":\"%s\"}\n",
   666	                    esc(label).c_str(), esc(model_path).c_str(), pn.c_str(), dn.c_str(), esc(conv.id).c_str(), turn, esc(e).c_str());
   667	            fflush(out);
   668	            failures++;
   669	        };
   670	        auto clear = [](Proc & x) { if (x.npu) x.npu->clear(); else llama_memory_clear(llama_get_memory(x.ctx), true); };
   671	        // A turn that would not fit is the end of that conversation: n_ctx for llama.cpp, and the
   672	        // NPU window with the prompt graph's padding when --window is given (to every process).
   673	        if ((P.npu || D.npu) && window != (int) (P.npu ? P.npu : D.npu)->window) {
   674	            fprintf(stderr, "--window must equal the NPU export's window (%zu)\n", (P.npu ? P.npu : D.npu)->window); return 2;
   675	        }
   676	        const int limit = window > 0 ? std::min(n_ctx, window) : n_ctx;
   677	        auto span = [&](size_t n) { return (int) ((n + pad_batch - 1) / pad_batch * pad_batch); };
   678	        for (auto & conv : convs) {
   679	            clear(P);
   680	            if (!same) clear(D);
   681	            std::vector<Msg> hist;
   682	            std::vector<llama_token> evaluated;  // exactly what the prefill context holds
   683	            int turn = 0;
   684	            for (size_t mi = 0; mi < conv.msgs.size() && turn < max_turns; mi++) {
   685	                hist.push_back(conv.msgs[mi]);
   686	                if (conv.msgs[mi].role != "user") continue;
   687	                std::string full = render(vmodel, hist, true);
   688	                if (full.empty()) { row_err(conv, turn, "chat template failed"); break; }
   689	                std::vector<llama_token> all = tokenize_prompt(vocab, full);
   690	                // Teacher forcing only works if the new prompt extends what is cached, token for token.
   691	                if (all.size() <= evaluated.size() || !std::equal(evaluated.begin(), evaluated.end(), all.begin())) {
   692	                    row_err(conv, turn, "token prefix changed"); break;
   693	                }
   694	                std::vector<llama_token> toks(all.begin() + evaluated.size(), all.end());
   695	                const int pos = (int) evaluated.size();
   696	                int ref_tokens = -1, turn_gen = gen;
   697	                if (gen_ref) {
   698	                    // The reply as plain text, without template markup; +1 stands for its end of turn.
   699	                    if (mi + 1 < conv.msgs.size() && conv.msgs[mi + 1].role == "assistant")
   700	                        ref_tokens = (int) tokenize(vocab, conv.msgs[mi + 1].content, false).size() + 1;
   701	                    turn_gen = ref_tokens < 0 ? std::min(gen, 64) : std::max(2, std::min(gen, ref_tokens));
   702	                }
   703	                if (pos + std::max((int) toks.size(), span(toks.size())) + turn_gen + 8 > limit) break;
   704	                std::string err;
   705	
   706	                // On the same llama.cpp processor a hybrid cannot drop the decoded tokens afterwards,
   707	                // so its state is saved before the prefill instead, outside both timed intervals.
   708	                // The NPU drops them from its attention caches; its conv states (if any) are copied
   709	                // after the prefill and put back after the decode, and that copy is subtracted.
   710	                const bool needs_snap = same && !P.npu && (llama_model_is_hybrid(P.model) || llama_model_is_recurrent(P.model));
   711	                snap.clear();
   712	                if (needs_snap) {
   713	                    snap.resize(llama_state_seq_get_size(P.ctx, 0));
   714	                    if (snap.empty() || llama_state_seq_get_data(P.ctx, snap.data(), snap.size(), 0) != snap.size()) {
   715	                        row_err(conv, turn, "snapshot failed"); break;
   716	                    }
   717	                }
   718	                if (P.npu && (int) P.npu->pos() != pos) { row_err(conv, turn, "npu position differs from the history"); break; }
   719	
   720	                Heat hb = heat();  // before the turn, outside its timing
   721	                long wall_ms = (long) std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(std::chrono::system_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count();
   722	                auto t_turn = clk::now();
   723	                auto t0 = clk::now();
   724	                bool ok = true;
   725	                llama_token first = 0;
   726	                double prompt_ms = 0;
   727	                if (P.npu) {
   728	                    const void * lg = P.npu->prefill_logits(std::vector<int32_t>(toks.begin(), toks.end()));
   729	                    prompt_ms = ms_since(t0);  // prompt evaluated, as llama.cpp's: argmax after
   730	                    if (!lg) { ok = false; err = "prefill failed: " + P.npu->error; } else first = (llama_token) P.npu->argmax(lg);
   731	                } else {
   732	                    ok = feed(P.ctx, toks, pos);
   733	                    const float * lg = ok ? llama_get_logits_ith(P.ctx, -1) : nullptr;
   734	                    prompt_ms = ms_since(t0);  // prompt evaluated and synchronised
   735	                    if (lg) first = argmax(P.ctx, n_vocab); else { ok = false; err = "prefill failed"; }
   736	                }
   737	                double prefill_ms = ms_since(t0);  // plus choosing the first token
   738	                double snap_ms = 0;
   739	                if (ok && same && P.npu && !P.npu->attention_only) { auto ts = clk::now(); P.npu->save_conv(); snap_ms = ms_since(ts); }
   740	
   741	                double get_ms = 0, set_ms = 0, clear_ms = 0; size_t xfer_bytes = 0;
   742	                if (ok && !same) {
   743	                    const size_t n_held = (size_t) pos + toks.size();
   744	                    // Inside turn_ms, as in pd_bench; timed on its own because it scales with the
   745	                    // context's capacity (2,304 cells here, 8,192 on Snapdragon) and not with the turn.
   746	                    if (!D.npu) { auto tc = clk::now(); llama_memory_clear(llama_get_memory(D.ctx), true); clear_ms = ms_since(tc); }
   747	                    // The buffer is sized before the clock starts, as pd_bench does.
   748	                    if (P.npu) state.reserve(P.npu->state_bytes(n_held));
   749	                    else { xfer_bytes = llama_state_seq_get_size(P.ctx, 0); if (state.size() < xfer_bytes) state.resize(xfer_bytes); }
   750	                    auto tg = clk::now();
   751	                    size_t wrote = 0;
   752	                    if (P.npu) {
   753	                        if (!P.npu->to_llama(D.layout, n_held, state)) { ok = false; err = "npu to llama: " + P.npu->error; }
   754	                        wrote = xfer_bytes = state.size();
   755	                    } else {
   756	                        wrote = llama_state_seq_get_data(P.ctx, state.data(), xfer_bytes, 0);
   757	                    }
   758	                    get_ms = ms_since(tg);
   759	                    auto ts = clk::now();
   760	                    if (ok && D.npu) {
   761	                        if (wrote != xfer_bytes || !D.npu->from_llama(state.data(), xfer_bytes, P.kind)) { ok = false; err = "llama to npu: " + D.npu->error; }
   762	                        else if (D.npu->pos() != n_held) { ok = false; err = "npu holds the wrong length after the handoff"; }
   763	                    } else if (ok) {
   764	                        size_t read = wrote == xfer_bytes ? llama_state_seq_set_data(D.ctx, state.data(), xfer_bytes, 0) : 0;
   765	                        if (xfer_bytes == 0 || wrote != xfer_bytes || read != xfer_bytes) { ok = false; err = "state transfer failed"; }
   766	                    }
   767	                    set_ms = ms_since(ts);
   768	                }
   769	
   770	                std::vector<llama_token> out_toks;
   771	                std::vector<double> step_ms;
   772	                std::vector<int> step_threads;
   773	                double decode_ms = 0; int steps = 0;
   774	                // Only a CPU decoder has threads to change; the switch is read before each step and
   775	                // inside its timing, so a cell that switches pays for the reads it makes.
   776	                const bool switching = paused_threads > 0 && dn == "cpu";
   777	                int cur_threads = -1;
   778	                if (ok) {
   779	                    out_toks.push_back(first);
   780	                    llama_batch b = llama_batch_init(1, 0, 1);
   781	                    auto td = clk::now();
   782	                    int p2 = pos + (int) toks.size();
   783	                    for (int g = 1; g < turn_gen; g++) {
   784	                        auto tstep = clk::now();
   785	                        if (D.npu) {
   786	                            int64_t nt = D.npu->step(out_toks.back());
   787	                            if (nt < 0) { ok = false; err = "decode failed: " + D.npu->error; break; }
   788	                            out_toks.push_back((llama_token) nt);
   789	                            p2++;
   790	                        } else {
   791	                            if (switching) {
   792	                                int pz = cpu_paused(switches);
   793	                                if (pz < 0) { ok = false; err = "pause switch unreadable"; break; }
   794	                                int want = pz ? paused_threads : threads;
   795	                                if (want != cur_threads) { llama_set_n_threads(D.ctx, want, want); cur_threads = want; }
   796	                            }
   797	                            b.n_tokens = 1; b.token[0] = out_toks.back(); b.pos[0] = p2++;
   798	                            b.n_seq_id[0] = 1; b.seq_id[0][0] = 0; b.logits[0] = 1;
   799	                            if (llama_decode(D.ctx, b) != 0) { ok = false; err = "decode failed"; break; }
   800	                            out_toks.push_back(argmax(D.ctx, n_vocab));
   801	                        }
   802	                        if (gen_ref) step_ms.push_back(ms_since(tstep));
   803	                        if (switching) step_threads.push_back(cur_threads);  // after success, so it lines up with step_ms
   804	                        steps++;
   805	                    }
   806	                    decode_ms = ms_since(td);
   807	                    llama_batch_free(b);
   808	                    // The next turn's prefill and warm-up use the cell's own count again.
   809	                    if (switching && cur_threads != threads) llama_set_n_threads(D.ctx, threads, threads);
   810	                }
   811	                double turn_ms = ms_since(t_turn) - snap_ms;  // prefill, first token, handoff (with its clear) and decode
   812	                Heat ht = heat(false);  // at the end of the turn, outside its timing: clocks only, the logger has the HAL
   813	                if (same && ok) {
   814	                    // Put the processor back to the dataset's history: drop the decoded tokens, or for
   815	                    // a llama.cpp hybrid restore the snapshot and read this turn's prompt again (untimed).
   816	                    if (P.npu) {
   817	                        if (!P.npu->keep_first((size_t) pos + toks.size())) { ok = false; err = "npu rollback failed"; }
   818	                        if (!P.npu->attention_only) P.npu->restore_conv();
   819	                    } else {
   820	                        llama_memory_t mem = llama_get_memory(P.ctx);
   821	                        if (!llama_memory_seq_rm(mem, 0, pos + (int) toks.size(), -1)) {
   822	                            if (snap.empty()) { ok = false; err = "cannot drop decoded tokens"; }
   823	                            llama_memory_clear(mem, true);
   824	                            if (llama_state_seq_set_data(P.ctx, snap.data(), snap.size(), 0) != snap.size() || !feed(P.ctx, toks, pos)) {

   827	                        }

        print('\\''rankings'\\'',[(a,['\\''cpu'\\'','\\''gpu'\\'','\\''npu'\\''][p]+'\\'':'\\''+['\\''cpu'\\'','\\''gpu'\\'','\\''npu'\\''][d],c['\\''order'\\''],c['\\''gate'\\'']) for a,p,d,c in cs])
files=['\\''mediatek/s4/results-s4.jsonl'\\'','\\''mediatek/s5/results-s5.jsonl'\\'','\\''mediatek/s5/results-s5b.jsonl'\\'','\\''mediatek/s6/results-s6.jsonl'\\'','\\''snapdragon/s24a/results-s24a.jsonl'\\'','\\''snapdragon/s24b/results-s24b.jsonl'\\'']
raw={}
for fn in files:
    rows=[]
    for i,l in enumerate((root/fn).read_text().splitlines(),1):
        if l.startswith('\\''{'\\''): r=json.loads(l); r['\\''_line'\\'']=i; r['\\''_file'\\'']=fn; rows.append(r)

        cs=collections.Counter(r['\\''prefill'\\'']+'\\'':'\\''+r['\\''decode'\\''] for r in ts)
        print(pathlib.Path(model).name,'\\''turncounts'\\'',dict(cs),'\\''errors'\\'',[(r['\\''_line'\\''],r['\\''kind'\\''],r.get('\\''error'\\'')) for r in rs if r.get('\\''error'\\'')],'\\''done'\\'',[(r['\\''_line'\\''],r.get('\\''failures'\\'')) for r in rs if r['\\''kind'\\'']=='\\''done'\\''])
        print('\\''gates'\\'',[(r['\\''_line'\\''],r['\\''prefill'\\'']+'\\'':'\\''+r['\\''decode'\\''],r['\\''gate'\\''],r['\\''waited_s'\\'']) for r in rs if r['\\''kind'\\'']=='\\''cooled'\\''])
        if ts: print('\\''cpu_top'\\'',ts[0]['\\''before_cpu_max_mhz'\\''])
mtk=[r for fn,rs in raw.items() if fn.startswith('\\''mediatek'\\'') for r in rs if r['\\''kind'\\'']=='\\''turn'\\'' and not r.get('\\''error'\\'')]
sd=[r for fn,rs in raw.items() if fn.startswith('\\''snapdragon'\\'') for r in rs if r['\\''kind'\\'']=='\\''turn'\\'' and not r.get('\\''error'\\'')]
for name in ['\\''Qwen3-0.6B-Q4_0.gguf'\\'','\\''Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct-Q4_0.gguf'\\'','\\''google_gemma-3-1b-it-Q4_0.gguf'\\'']:
    T=[r for r in mtk if pathlib.Path(r['\\''model'\\'']).name==name]
    S=[r for r in sd if pathlib.Path(r['\\''model'\\'']).name==name and (r['\\''label'\\'']=='\\''s24a'\\'' if name.startswith('\\''Qwen'\\'') else r['\\''label'\\'']=='\\''s24b'\\'')]
    work=lambda r:(r['\\''pos'\\''],r['\\''new_tokens'\\''],r['\\''decode_steps'\\''],r['\\''turn_gen'\\''],r['\\''ref_tokens'\\''])
    tm=collections.defaultdict(set)
    for r in T: tm[(r['\\''conv'\\''],r['\\''turn'\\''])].add(work(r))
    mismatch=[(r['\\''_file'\\''],r['\\''_line'\\''],r['\\''conv'\\''],r['\\''turn'\\''],work(r),tm[(r['\\''conv'\\''],r['\\''turn'\\''])]) for r in S if work(r) not in tm[(r['\\''conv'\\''],r['\\''turn'\\''])]]
    print('\\''CROSS_WORK'\\'',name,'\\''tablet_rows'\\'',len(T),'\\''S24_rows'\\'',len(S),'\\''S24_turns'\\'',len({(r['\\''conv'\\''],r['\\''turn'\\'']) for r in S}),'\\''mismatch'\\'',mismatch[:10])
qT=[r for r in mtk if '\\''Qwen3-0.6B-'\\'' in r['\\''model'\\'']]
qS=[r for r in sd if '\\''Qwen3-0.6B-'\\'' in r['\\''model'\\'']]
for label,rows in [('\\''tablet'\\'',qT),('\\''S24'\\'',qS)]:
    print('\\''QWEN'\\'',label)

        rate={}
        for p in ['\\''cpu'\\'','\\''gpu'\\'','\\''npu'\\'']:
            rs=[r for r in rows if r[phase]==p]
            rate[p]=sum(r['\\''new_tokens'\\'' if phase=='\\''prefill'\\'' else '\\''decode_steps'\\''] for r in rs)/sum(r['\\''prompt_ms'\\'' if phase=='\\''prefill'\\'' else '\\''decode_ms'\\''] for r in rs)*1000
        print(phase,rate,'\\''N/C'\\'',rate['\\''npu'\\'']/rate['\\''cpu'\\''],'\\''G/C'\\'',rate['\\''gpu'\\'']/rate['\\''cpu'\\''])
    for r in rows:
        slow=[(i+1,x) for i,x in enumerate(r['\\''step_ms'\\'']) if x>500 and r['\\''decode'\\'']=='\\''cpu'\\'']
        if slow: print('\\''SLOW'\\'',r['\\''_file'\\''],r['\\''_line'\\''],r['\\''prefill'\\'']+'\\'':'\\''+r['\\''decode'\\''],r['\\''conv'\\''],r['\\''turn'\\''],'\\''slow'\\'',slow,'\\''total_s'\\'',sum(x for i,x in slow)/1000,'\\''median_ms'\\'',statistics.median(r['\\''step_ms'\\'']))

files={'\\''Qwen tablet'\\'':['\\''mediatek/s5/results-s5.jsonl'\\'','\\''mediatek/s5/results-s5b.jsonl'\\''],'\\''Qwen S24'\\'':['\\''snapdragon/s24a/results-s24a.jsonl'\\''],'\\''Gemma tablet'\\'':['\\''mediatek/s6/results-s6.jsonl'\\''],'\\''Llama tablet'\\'':['\\''mediatek/s4/results-s4.jsonl'\\'']}
for name,fs in files.items():
    model='\\''Qwen3-0.6B'\\'' if '\\''Qwen'\\'' in name else '\\''google_gemma-3-1b'\\'' if '\\''Gemma'\\'' in name else '\\''Llama-3.2-1B'\\''
    turns=[dict(json.loads(l),_line=i,_file=fn) for fn in fs for i,l in enumerate(pathlib.Path(fn).read_text().splitlines(),1) if l.startswith('\\''{'\\'')]
    turns=[r for r in turns if r.get('\\''kind'\\'')=='\\''turn'\\'' and not r.get('\\''error'\\'') and model in r['\\''model'\\'']]
    fn=pathlib.Path(fs[0]).parent/('\\''sdlog-s24a.txt'\\'' if '\\''S24'\\'' in name else '\\''mtklog-'\\''+pathlib.Path(fs[0]).parent.name+'\\''.txt'\\'')
    ls=fn.read_text().splitlines()
    head=next(l for l in ls if l.startswith('\\''epoch'\\'')).split('\\''|'\\'')[0].split()
    pols=[(h[1:-4],i) for i,h in enumerate(head) if re.fullmatch(r'\\''p\\d+_max'\\'',h)]
    tops={p:float(s.split('\\''/'\\'')[1])*1000 for p,s in (v.split('\\'':'\\'') for v in turns[0]['\\''before_cpu_max_mhz'\\''].split('\\'','\\''))}
    logs=[]
    for lineno,l in enumerate(ls,1):
        if not re.match(r'\\''"'^'"\\d'\\'',l):continue
        f=l.split('\\''|'\\'')[0].split(); h=l.split('\\''|'\\'')[1].split() if '\\''|'\\'' in l else None
        logs.append((float(f[0]),f,h,lineno))
    convs=list(dict.fromkeys(r['\\''conv'\\''] for r in turns))
    sources={c:next(r['\\''source'\\''] for r in turns if r['\\''conv'\\'']==c) for c in convs}
    chosen=[]; counts=collections.Counter()
    for c in convs:
        if counts[sources[c]]<2: chosen.append(c); counts[sources[c]]+=1
    subsets=[('\\''full'\\'',turns)]
    if '\\''Gemma'\\'' in name: subsets.append(('\\''six conversations'\\'', [r for r in turns if r['\\''conv'\\''] in chosen]))
    for sub,rs in subsets:
        t0=min(r['\\''wall_ms'\\'']/1000 for r in rs); t1=max((r['\\''wall_ms'\\'']+r['\\''turn_ms'\\''])/1000 for r in rs)
        intervals=sorted((r['\\''wall_ms'\\'']/1000,(r['\\''wall_ms'\\'']+r['\\''turn_ms'\\''])/1000) for r in rs)
        window=[r for r in logs if t0<=r[0]<=t1]
        active=[r for r in window if any(a<=r[0]<=b for a,b in intervals)]
        nonactive=[r for r in window if not any(a<=r[0]<=b for a,b in intervals)]
        def summary(log):
            hs=[r[2] for r in log if r[2]]
            top=sum(all(float(r[1][i])>=tops[p]-1000 for p,i in pols) for r in log)
            return {'\\''samples'\\'':len(log),'\\''hal_samples'\\'':len(hs),'\\''top'\\'':top,'\\''top_pct'\\'':round(100*top/len(log),3),'\\''critical'\\'':sum(float(h[0])>=4 for h in hs),'\\''critical_pct'\\'':round(100*sum(float(h[0])>=4 for h in hs)/len(hs),3),'\\''max_ap'\\'':max(float(h[1]) for h in hs),'\\''lowest_ghz'\\'':[round(min(float(r[1][i]) for r in log)/1e6,4) for p,i in pols]}
        print(name,sub,'\\''turns'\\'',len(rs),'\\''keys'\\'',len(set((r['\\''conv'\\''],r['\\''turn'\\'']) for r in rs)),'\\''convs'\\'',sorted(set(r['\\''conv'\\''] for r in rs)),'\\''policies'\\'',pols,'\\''tops'\\'',tops)
        print('\\''window_seconds'\\'',round(t1-t0,3),'\\''timed_seconds'\\'',round(sum(r['\\''turn_ms'\\''] for r in rs)/1000,3),'\\''window_lines'\\'',window[0][3],window[-1][3])
        print('\\''window'\\'',summary(window),'\\''active'\\'',summary(active),'\\''nonactive'\\'',summary(nonactive))

codex
I found a heat-window bug: the generator includes every logger sample between a model’s first and last turn, including cooling waits and, for a subset, excluded turns. For Qwen, that raises the S24’s “at top” figure from 23.6% during timed turns to the displayed 33.0%. I also checked the completion guard in memory: it withholds the current unfinished S24 run, but can accept a finished run with failed turns by silently shrinking the comparison set.

   388	// Feeds tokens in n_batch pieces; logits only for the last one.
   389	static bool feed(llama_context * ctx, const std::vector<llama_token> & toks, int pos0) {
   390	    int nb = llama_n_batch(ctx);
   391	    llama_batch b = llama_batch_init(nb, 0, 1);
   392	    for (size_t i = 0; i < toks.size(); i += nb) {
   393	        b.n_tokens = 0;
   394	        for (size_t j = i; j < toks.size() && j < i + nb; j++) {
   395	            b.token[b.n_tokens] = toks[j]; b.pos[b.n_tokens] = pos0 + (int) j;
   396	            b.n_seq_id[b.n_tokens] = 1; b.seq_id[b.n_tokens][0] = 0;
   397	            b.logits[b.n_tokens] = (j == toks.size() - 1);
   398	            b.n_tokens++;
   399	        }
   400	        if (llama_decode(ctx, b) != 0) { llama_batch_free(b); return false; }
   401	    }
   402	    llama_batch_free(b);
   403	    return true;
   404	}

   406	static llama_token argmax(llama_context * ctx, int n_vocab) {
   407	    const float * l = llama_get_logits_ith(ctx, -1);
   408	    int best = 0;
   409	    for (int i = 1; i < n_vocab; i++) if (l[i] > l[best]) best = i;
   410	    return best;
   411	}

   413	// The NPU reads a run of a token the way warm() does for llama.cpp: graphs and buffers are
   414	// touched once before anything is timed.
   415	static bool warm_npu(npu::Engine & e) {
   416	    std::vector<int32_t> t(512, 0);
   417	    bool ok = e.prefill(t) >= 0;
   418	    for (int i = 0; i < 8 && ok; i++) ok = e.step(0) >= 0;
   419	    e.clear();
   420	    return ok;
   421	}
